    <shape tag="shape" id="14" name="圆角矩形标注 13" presetGeometry="wedgeRoundRectCallout">
      <text_box tag="text_box" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">其他方法：</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">1</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">）仅考虑</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="true" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="true" underline="none">Bel</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">因素，</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">即</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="true" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="true" underline="none">DF=0</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">此时，</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="true" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="true" underline="none">ci-0 = 0.813 </plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="true" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="true" underline="none">&lt; 0.883</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">2</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">）考虑</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="true" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="true" underline="none">Bel</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">和</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="true" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="true" underline="none">Pls</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">的</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">平均值</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">，</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
